- Menu: [0] Kaito, Bane of Nightmares 3/4 (A); [1] Deep-Cavern Bat 1/1 (A); [2] Preacher of the Schism 2/4 (B); [3] Preacher of the Schism 2/4 (A); [4] Enduring Curiosity 4/3 (A); [5] Preacher of the Schism 2/4 (A); [6] Preacher of the Schism 2/4 (B); [7] Floodpits Drowner 2/1 (B)
